2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAura is a self-hosted, multi-user AI agent. Its Go binary hosts the runtime, tools,\nCLI, Telegram gateway, and embedded web cockpit; each person signs in to their own\nidentity, with their own memory database, workspace and sandbox. Docker Compose runs\nPostgres, ArcadeDB, Garage, embedding, ingestion, web search, voice and the bundled\nintegrations alongside it.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe model is chosen in the cockpit settings: OpenRouter (the default route), a\nChatGPT plan, the bundled local llama.cpp server, or Ollama. Local storage does not\nmake cloud inference offline: a cloud provider receives the context sent to its\nmodel; with a local server nothing leaves the host for inference.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eHardware:\u003c/strong\u003e a mini PC with 16 GB of RAM is enough. The default stack measured\n7 GB with speech-to-text and text-to-speech running on a 16 GB mini PC\n(2026-09-02). No local LLM runs by default: inference goes to the provider you choose.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv align=\"center\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003ca target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/chetto1983/Aura/blob/master/public/demo.gif\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"/chetto1983/Aura/raw/master/public/demo.gif\" alt=\"Aura cockpit: the agent stores a birthday in its memory graph and schedules a reminder, then a new chat answers from memory\" width=\"820\" data-animated-image=\"\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003csub\u003eA real run on a local stack: Aura stores the fact in its memory graph, loads the deferred\n\u003ccode\u003etask\u003c/code\u003e tool and schedules the reminder; a new chat then answers from memory, with\nprovenance. Model replies were written by Claude through an OpenAI-compatible endpoint;\nwaiting time is trimmed.\u003c/sub\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStreaming agent loop\u003c/strong\u003e with shared step/time budgets and repeated-call controls to bound work.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDeferred tools and \u003ccode\u003etool_search\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — discover tools and load their schemas when needed, including tools from mounted MCP servers.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAdaptive reasoning router\u003c/strong\u003e — selects reasoning effort using the configured classifier and the active model's supported capabilities.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFull host terminal + filesystem tools\u003c/strong\u003e — real operating power, with destructive-command approval gates and secret redaction.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGraph-native memory\u003c/strong\u003e — facts, sources and validity windows in ArcadeDB; temporal paths return supporting evidence. Postgres-authoritative conversations have a derived recall projection and managed context compaction.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDocument retrieval\u003c/strong\u003e — indexed passages with source hashes and citations, plus access to the original file for calculations and whole-file tasks.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSelf-extension\u003c/strong\u003e — author and run skills, use bundled memory/PIM/WhatsApp integrations, and connect additional MCP servers.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eScheduler and self wake-ups\u003c/strong\u003e — one \u003ccode\u003etask\u003c/code\u003e tool (\u003ccode\u003eat | every | cron\u003c/code\u003e) for reminders and \u003ccode\u003eagent_job\u003c/code\u003e runs, with job policy, operator controls and outcomes delivered to the owning conversation.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePer-identity sandbox\u003c/strong\u003e — a full-capability box per operator (opt-in \u003ccode\u003esandbox\u003c/code\u003e profile; gVisor \u003ccode\u003erunsc\u003c/code\u003e on native Linux), with deliverables handed back over the channel (\u003ccode\u003esend_file\u003c/code\u003e), never as a path.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMulti-user\u003c/strong\u003e — Authula sign-in (password, plus a TOTP step for accounts enrolled in it), one isolated ArcadeDB database per identity enforced by the server, capability grants, and an admin audit view.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMulti-channel\u003c/strong\u003e — CLI REPL, Telegram (voice/photo/docs/HITL), and a web cockpit over AG-UI/SSE with mid-turn steering, approvals, voice input/output, and live settings.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStudio\u003c/strong\u003e — image and video generation, photo and video editing, and a multi-track video editor, all in the cockpit (\u003ca href=\"#studio\"\u003edetails\u003c/a\u003e).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBundled integrations\u003c/strong\u003e — calendar/e-mail (PIM MCP, OAuth providers), WhatsApp (unofficial client), web search through a bundled SearXNG, snapshot share links to a conversation, and Cloudflare remote access.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAura is a self-hosted agent runtime packaged as a Docker Compose appliance. The\ndefault stack brings up Aura (with its migration one-shot), Postgres, ArcadeDB and its\nMCP, Garage, the local embedding sidecar, document ingestion, SearXNG, speech-to-text\nand text-to-speech, the PIM and WhatsApp MCP sidecars, the Cloudflare tunnel\nsupervisor (idle until enabled), and Caddy in front of the Authula sign-in. Compose\nprofiles add the rest: \u003ccode\u003elocalllm\u003c/code\u003e (a llama.cpp server with Gemma 4 12B QAT), \u003ccode\u003eocr\u003c/code\u003e,\n\u003ccode\u003eobservability\u003c/code\u003e (Prometheus, Tempo, Grafana) and \u003ccode\u003esandbox\u003c/code\u003e (the Docker socket proxy\nfor per-identity boxes).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eQuick Start\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca \u003ccode\u003eghcr.io/chetto1983/aura:\u0026lt;tag\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e and the binary archives are published by\nthe \u003ccode\u003eRelease\u003c/code\u003e workflow on a \u003ccode\u003ev*\u003c/code\u003e tag, and only after the exact-SHA \u003cem\u003eProduction\nReadiness\u003c/em\u003e check passed for that commit (\u003ca href=\"/chetto1983/Aura/blob/master/docs/release-readiness.md\"\u003edocs/release-readiness.md\u003c/a\u003e).\nCheck the\n\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/chetto1983/Aura/releases\"\u003eReleases page\u003c/a\u003e for the current tag\n(\u003ccode\u003ev1.0.2-rc1\u003c/code\u003e is the latest) and use it as \u003ccode\u003evX.Y.Z\u003c/code\u003e below. Independently of\nreleases, every master push publishes the moving \u003ccode\u003eghcr.io/chetto1983/aura:edge\u003c/code\u003e\nimage (plus an immutable \u003ccode\u003emaster-\u0026lt;sha\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e tag) — the continuous-delivery channel a\ndefault install tracks.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eLinux or macOS\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-linux-or-macos\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Linux or macOS\" href=\"#linux-or-macos\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe interactive installer supports local installation or a Linux target over SSH:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"npx create-aura-appliance\nnpx create-aura-appliance --mode remote\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003enpx create-aura-appliance\nnpx create-aura-appliance --mode remote\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIt requires Node.js \u003cstrong\u003e22.13 or newer\u003c/strong\u003e on the workstation. The target needs at least\n\u003cstrong\u003e4 CPU cores, 14 GiB usable RAM, and 20 GiB free disk\u003c/strong\u003e; documents, models and backup\nretention need additional capacity. The installer detects the embedding backend on the\ntarget: CUDA for an NVIDIA GPU Docker can drive, otherwise Vulkan for an Intel or AMD GPU\nexposing \u003ccode\u003e/dev/dri\u003c/code\u003e, otherwise CPU. See the \u003ca href=\"/chetto1983/Aura/blob/master/packages/create-aura/README.md\"\u003einstaller guide\u003c/a\u003e\nfor supported targets and prerequisites. The npm installer carries its own payload.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe source-hosted installer remains available:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eInstall Docker, then run the installer. One command on a machine with Node 18+\n(\u003ccode\u003enpx\u003c/code\u003e fetches the repo and runs \u003ccode\u003escripts/install.sh\u003c/code\u003e):\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"sudo npx github:chetto1983/Aura -- --appliance\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003esudo npx github:chetto1983/Aura -- --appliance\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eor the curl equivalent of the same script — use \u003ccode\u003emaster\u003c/code\u003e to track the edge\nchannel, or a release tag \u003ccode\u003evX.Y.Z\u003c/code\u003e to pin:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/chetto1983/Aura/master/scripts/install.sh | sudo bash -s -- --appliance\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003ecurl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/chetto1983/Aura/master/scripts/install.sh \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e|\u003c/span\u003e sudo bash -s -- --appliance\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe installer checks hardware, creates \u003ccode\u003e.env\u003c/code\u003e with generated \u003ccode\u003ePOSTGRES_PASSWORD\u003c/code\u003e,\nthe three \u003ccode\u003eARCADEDB_*\u003c/code\u003e secrets, and \u003ccode\u003eAURA_ACCESS_TOKEN\u003c/code\u003e, downloads the Compose/Caddy assets, and\nstarts the stack. Re-running it keeps an existing \u003ccode\u003e.env\u003c/code\u003e intact. A master/edge\ninstall points \u003ccode\u003e.env\u003c/code\u003e at the \u003ccode\u003e:edge\u003c/code\u003e moving tags, and \u003ccode\u003e--appliance\u003c/code\u003e also enables\nthe \u003ccode\u003eaura-image-update\u003c/code\u003e systemd timer: from then on the machine re-pulls aura and\nits MCP sidecars from GHCR on its own, migrations and Compose payload included, with\nno operator involved. Without \u003ccode\u003e--appliance\u003c/code\u003e (no systemd units, no timer), the stack still\nstarts; updates stay manual.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe default stack also starts the Cloudflare supervisor healthy-idle. Enable it after setup in\n\u003cstrong\u003eSettings \u0026gt; Remote access\u003c/strong\u003e; the installer requires no Cloudflare credential. Archives\nlive in the separate \u003ccode\u003eaura-arcadedb-backups\u003c/code\u003e volume. The configuration sets\n\u003ccode\u003emaxFiles=60\u003c/code\u003e and tiered hourly/daily/weekly/monthly retention of 24/7/4/6.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe database and backup volumes are separate, but both are on the same host by\ndefault. This is a dated recovery check,\nnot a complete host-loss rehearsal or an RPO/RTO guarantee. <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Aura</h1><a id="user-content-aura" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Aura" href="#aura"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>A local-first, provider-neutral AI agent platform — in Go.</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">An agent for ongoing work: tools, document retrieval, temporal memory, scheduled
jobs, and a web cockpit on infrastructure you control.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/chetto1983/Aura/actions/workflows/ci.yml"><img src="https://github.com/chetto1983/Aura/actions/workflows/ci.yml/badge.svg?branch=master" alt="CI" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<a href="https://github.com/chetto1983/Aura/actions/workflows/codeql.yml"><img src="https://github.com/chetto1983/Aura/actions/workflows/codeql.yml/badge.svg?branch=master" alt="CodeQL" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What is Aura?</h2><a id="user-content-what-is-aura" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What is Aura?" href="#what-is-aura"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Aura is a self-hosted, multi-user AI agent. Its Go binary hosts the runtime, tools,
CLI, Telegram gateway, and embedded web cockpit; each person signs in to their own
identity, with their own memory database, workspace and sandbox. Docker Compose runs
Postgres, ArcadeDB, Garage, embedding, ingestion, web search, voice and the bundled
integrations alongside it.</p>
<p dir="auto">The model is chosen in the cockpit settings: OpenRouter (the default route), a
ChatGPT plan, the bundled local llama.cpp server, or Ollama. Local storage does not
make cloud inference offline: a cloud provider receives the context sent to its
model; with a local server nothing leaves the host for inference.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Hardware:</strong> a mini PC with 16 GB of RAM is enough. The default stack measured
7 GB with speech-to-text and text-to-speech running on a 16 GB mini PC
(2026-09-02). No local LLM runs by default: inference goes to the provider you choose.</p>
</blockquote>
<div align="center" dir="auto">
<a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/chetto1983/Aura/blob/master/public/demo.gif"><img src="/chetto1983/Aura/raw/master/public/demo.gif" alt="Aura cockpit: the agent stores a birthday in its memory graph and schedules a reminder, then a new chat answers from memory" width="820" data-animated-image="" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<p dir="auto"><sub>A real run on a local stack: Aura stores the fact in its memory graph, loads the deferred
<code>task</code> tool and schedules the reminder; a new chat then answers from memory, with
provenance. Model replies were written by Claude through an OpenAI-compatible endpoint;
waiting time is trimmed.</sub></p>
</div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">At a glance</h2><a id="user-content-at-a-glance" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: At a glance" href="#at-a-glance"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Key features</h2><a id="user-content-key-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Key features" href="#key-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Streaming agent loop</strong> with shared step/time budgets and repeated-call controls to bound work.</li>
<li><strong>Deferred tools and <code>tool_search</code></strong> — discover tools and load their schemas when needed, including tools from mounted MCP servers.</li>
<li><strong>Adaptive reasoning router</strong> — selects reasoning effort using the configured classifier and the active model's supported capabilities.</li>
<li><strong>Full host terminal + filesystem tools</strong> — real operating power, with destructive-command approval gates and secret redaction.</li>
<li><strong>Graph-native memory</strong> — facts, sources and validity windows in ArcadeDB; temporal paths return supporting evidence. Postgres-authoritative conversations have a derived recall projection and managed context compaction.</li>
<li><strong>Document retrieval</strong> — indexed passages with source hashes and citations, plus access to the original file for calculations and whole-file tasks.</li>
<li><strong>Self-extension</strong> — author and run skills, use bundled memory/PIM/WhatsApp integrations, and connect additional MCP servers.</li>
<li><strong>Scheduler and self wake-ups</strong> — one <code>task</code> tool (<code>at | every | cron</code>) for reminders and <code>agent_job</code> runs, with job policy, operator controls and outcomes delivered to the owning conversation.</li>
<li><strong>Per-identity sandbox</strong> — a full-capability box per operator (opt-in <code>sandbox</code> profile; gVisor <code>runsc</code> on native Linux), with deliverables handed back over the channel (<code>send_file</code>), never as a path.</li>
<li><strong>Multi-user</strong> — Authula sign-in (password, plus a TOTP step for accounts enrolled in it), one isolated ArcadeDB database per identity enforced by the server, capability grants, and an admin audit view.</li>
<li><strong>Multi-channel</strong> — CLI REPL, Telegram (voice/photo/docs/HITL), and a web cockpit over AG-UI/SSE with mid-turn steering, approvals, voice input/output, and live settings.</li>
<li><strong>Studio</strong> — image and video generation, photo and video editing, and a multi-track video editor, all in the cockpit (<a href="#studio">details</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Bundled integrations</strong> — calendar/e-mail (PIM MCP, OAuth providers), WhatsApp (unofficial client), web search through a bundled SearXNG, snapshot share links to a conversation, and Cloudflare remote access.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Studio</h2><a id="user-content-studio" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Studio" href="#studio"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The cockpit's creative workspace, per identity.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Generate images and video</strong> from a prompt over OpenRouter's media models. The model
picker shows each model's price (per image, per second or per million output tokens)
and the estimated cost before you press Generate. Options cover resolution, aspect
ratio, seed, and duration and sound for video; advanced inputs take a start frame, an
end frame and reference images from your library. Every generation lands in a
searchable history you can reuse or download. Generation needs the OpenRouter route.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Edit photos and clips</strong> in the browser: a photo editor (Filerobot) and a quick video
editor (trim, crop, rotate, audio) for any image or clip in a chat or in the Garage
library.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Multi-track video editor</strong>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>a video lane plus overlay lanes for titles and images, with transitions between clips;</li>
<li>per-clip transform (fill, fit, crop, flip, rotate), adjustments (opacity, brightness,
contrast, saturation, hue, blur), animations and speed;</li>
<li>audio lanes for an uploaded sound, a recorded voice, a text read aloud, or the sound
extracted from a clip, with noise reduction, fades and automatic ducking under speech;</li>
<li>undo and redo, saved projects, a mobile layout, and an export rendered in the browser
(video, or the audio alone as WAV).</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">A generated video opens in the editor with one click.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How Aura compares</h2><a id="user-content-how-aura-compares" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How Aura compares" href="#how-aura-compares"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Checked on 2026-10-03 against each project's own documentation. Open WebUI and
LibreChat are mature, much larger projects; this table shows where Aura differs, not
that it is ahead.</p>

</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Choose Open WebUI or LibreChat for a polished multi-model chat front end with SSO and
a large ecosystem. Choose Aura for a long-running personal agent that remembers over
time, works on a schedule and reaches you on Telegram or WhatsApp.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Architecture (one screen)</h2><a id="user-content-architecture-one-screen" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Architecture (one screen)" href="#architecture-one-screen"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Deployment (Docker Compose appliance)</h2><a id="user-content-deployment-docker-compose-appliance" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deployment (Docker Compose appliance)" href="#deployment-docker-compose-appliance"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Aura is a self-hosted agent runtime packaged as a Docker Compose appliance. The
default stack brings up Aura (with its migration one-shot), Postgres, ArcadeDB and its
MCP, Garage, the local embedding sidecar, document ingestion, SearXNG, speech-to-text
and text-to-speech, the PIM and WhatsApp MCP sidecars, the Cloudflare tunnel
supervisor (idle until enabled), and Caddy in front of the Authula sign-in. Compose
profiles add the rest: <code>localllm</code> (a llama.cpp server with Gemma 4 12B QAT), <code>ocr</code>,
<code>observability</code> (Prometheus, Tempo, Grafana) and <code>sandbox</code> (the Docker socket proxy
for per-identity boxes).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Releases.</strong> <code>ghcr.io/chetto1983/aura:&lt;tag&gt;</code> and the binary archives are published by
the <code>Release</code> workflow on a <code>v*</code> tag, and only after the exact-SHA <em>Production
Readiness</em> check passed for that commit (<a href="/chetto1983/Aura/blob/master/docs/release-readiness.md">docs/release-readiness.md</a>).
Check the
<a href="https://github.com/chetto1983/Aura/releases">Releases page</a> for the current tag
(<code>v1.0.2-rc1</code> is the latest) and use it as <code>vX.Y.Z</code> below. Independently of
releases, every master push publishes the moving <code>ghcr.io/chetto1983/aura:edge</code>
image (plus an immutable <code>master-&lt;sha&gt;</code> tag) — the continuous-delivery channel a
default install tracks.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Linux or macOS</h3><a id="user-content-linux-or-macos" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Linux or macOS" href="#linux-or-macos"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The interactive installer supports local installation or a Linux target over SSH:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npx create-aura-appliance
npx create-aura-appliance --mode remote"><pre>npx create-aura-appliance
npx create-aura-appliance --mode remote</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">It requires Node.js <strong>22.13 or newer</strong> on the workstation. The target needs at least
<strong>4 CPU cores, 14 GiB usable RAM, and 20 GiB free disk</strong>; documents, models and backup
retention need additional capacity. The installer detects the embedding backend on the
target: CUDA for an NVIDIA GPU Docker can drive, otherwise Vulkan for an Intel or AMD GPU
exposing <code>/dev/dri</code>, otherwise CPU. See the <a href="/chetto1983/Aura/blob/master/packages/create-aura/README.md">installer guide</a>
for supported targets and prerequisites. The npm installer carries its own payload.</p>
<p dir="auto">The source-hosted installer remains available:</p>
<p dir="auto">Install Docker, then run the installer. One command on a machine with Node 18+
(<code>npx</code> fetches the repo and runs <code>scripts/install.sh</code>):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="sudo npx github:chetto1983/Aura -- --appliance"><pre>sudo npx github:chetto1983/Aura -- --appliance</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">or the curl equivalent of the same script — use <code>master</code> to track the edge
channel, or a release tag <code>vX.Y.Z</code> to pin:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/chetto1983/Aura/master/scripts/install.sh | sudo bash -s -- --appliance"><pre>curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/chetto1983/Aura/master/scripts/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> sudo bash -s -- --appliance</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The installer checks hardware, creates <code>.env</code> with generated <code>POSTGRES_PASSWORD</code>,
the three <code>ARCADEDB_*</code> secrets, and <code>AURA_ACCESS_TOKEN</code>, downloads the Compose/Caddy assets, and
starts the stack. Re-running it keeps an existing <code>.env</code> intact. A master/edge
install points <code>.env</code> at the <code>:edge</code> moving tags, and <code>--appliance</code> also enables
the <code>aura-image-update</code> systemd timer: from then on the machine re-pulls aura and
its MCP sidecars from GHCR on its own, migrations and Compose payload included, with
no operator involved. Without <code>--appliance</code> (no systemd units, no timer), the stack still
starts; updates stay manual.</p>
<p dir="auto">The default stack also starts the Cloudflare supervisor healthy-idle. Enable it after setup in
<strong>Settings &gt; Remote access</strong>; the installer requires no Cloudflare credential. Existing edge
appliances receive the sidecar through the payload updater without replacing database volumes.
See <a href="/chetto1983/Aura/blob/master/docs/cloudflare-remote-access.md">Cloudflare Remote Access</a> for registered-domain prerequisites,
Access OTP, organization WARP, token refresh and safe disable/delete.</p>
<p dir="auto">Add <code>--gvisor</code> on native Linux Docker hosts that should run Aura under <code>runsc</code>.
Docker Desktop is intentionally not supported for that isolation tier.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Windows</h3><a id="user-content-windows" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Windows" href="#windows"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use Docker Desktop and the shipped Compose files. From PowerShell in the Aura
checkout or release directory:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="function New-Hex { -join ((1..32) | ForEach-Object { '{0:x2}' -f (Get-Random -Maximum 256) }) }
@&quot;
POSTGRES_PASSWORD=$(New-Hex)
POSTGRES_USER=aura
POSTGRES_DB=aura
ARCADEDB_PASSWORD=$(New-Hex)

<span class="pl-s">AURA_AUTHULA_SECRET=<span class="pl-k"><span class="pl-pse">$</span></span><span class="pl-pse">(</span><span class="pl-c1">New-Hex</span><span class="pl-pse">)</span></span>

<span class="pl-s">AURA_GARAGE_ADMIN_TOKEN=<span class="pl-k"><span class="pl-pse">$</span></span><span class="pl-pse">(</span><span class="pl-c1">New-Hex</span><span class="pl-pse">)</span></span>
<span class="pl-s">AURA_BACKUP_DIR=./backups</span>
<span class="pl-s">AURA_EMBED_REVISION=0f741b5a6585bd53aeb15cd1372c56f2a0f65e12</span>
<span class="pl-s">AURA_EMBED_FINGERPRINT=b5ce9d77a3fc4b3b39ccb5643c36777911cc4eb46a66962eadfa3f5f60490d63</span>
<span class="pl-s">AURA_EMBED_NGL=99</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"@</span></span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">Set-Content</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Path .env <span class="pl-k">-</span>Encoding ascii

docker run <span class="pl-k">--</span>rm <span class="pl-k">--</span>gpus all nvidia<span class="pl-k">/</span>cuda:<span class="pl-c1">12.8</span>.<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-k">-</span>base<span class="pl-k">-</span>ubuntu24.<span class="pl-c1">04</span> nvidia<span class="pl-k">-</span>smi
docker compose up <span class="pl-k">-</span>d</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This <code>.env</code> targets an NVIDIA GPU: the embedding sidecar reserves one, so fix
Docker/NVIDIA before starting Aura if the <code>nvidia-smi</code> container check fails. Without
an NVIDIA GPU, run embeddings on the CPU instead: set <code>AURA_EMBED_NGL=0</code> and
<code>COMPOSE_FILE=compose.yaml;compose.cpu.yaml</code> (<code>;</code> is Compose's path separator on
Windows), which drops the GPU reservation and selects the CPU build of the pinned
llama.cpp server.</p>
<p dir="auto">The model route, the OpenRouter key and the Telegram bot token are not <code>.env</code>
settings: choose them in the first-run web setup, and Aura keeps them in
<code>aura.settings</code>. For local development images, replace <code>AURA_IMAGE</code> with
<code>aura:local</code> after building the image.</p>
<p dir="auto">Postgres 18 is the default Compose image for new installs. When upgrading an
existing Aura deployment from Postgres 17, migrate the data with <code>pg_dump</code> /
<code>pg_restore</code> or <code>pg_upgrade</code>; a Postgres 18 container cannot reuse a Postgres 17
data volume directly.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Access</h3><a id="user-content-access" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Access" href="#access"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Aura listens on loopback; Caddy serves the cockpit on HTTPS at <code>https://&lt;host&gt;</code>, behind
the Authula sign-in. On a fresh install the sign-in page offers <strong>Create first user</strong>:
that account is the operator, and after signing in the cockpit's first-run setup
finishes the configuration. The Telegram bot is connected through the setup wizard,
gated by the access token the installer prints:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="https://&lt;host&gt;/setup/?token=&lt;AURA_ACCESS_TOKEN&gt;"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>https://&lt;host&gt;/setup/?token=&lt;AURA_ACCESS_TOKEN&gt;
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Caddy uses <code>tls internal</code>. Browsers on other LAN machines will warn until they
trust the local CA root from the <code>caddy-data</code> volume:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker compose exec caddy cat /data/caddy/pki/authorities/local/root.crt &gt; aura-caddy-root.crt"><pre>docker compose <span class="pl-c1">exec</span> caddy cat /data/caddy/pki/authorities/local/root.crt <span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> aura-caddy-root.crt</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Trust on the Ubuntu server does not make remote browsers trust that CA. Cloudflare named-tunnel
hostnames use the public edge certificate; direct port 443 bypasses Cloudflare Access and retains
Authula. Quick Tunnels are temporary testing only and cannot validate Aura chat because they do
not support SSE.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Updates</h2><a id="user-content-updates" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Updates" href="#updates"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">An edge appliance installed with <code>--appliance</code> updates itself: the
<code>aura-image-update.timer</code> (5-minute cadence, flock-guarded) pulls the moving
tags and recreates only what changed, running migrations first. The aura image
also carries the installation payload — the Compose files, the updater and its
units, the sidecar configuration — and each tick installs whatever differs from
<code>/opt/aura</code> (backing up what it replaces under <code>backups/payload-*</code>) and brings
the whole stack up on it. A version pin changed in <code>compose.yaml</code> therefore
reaches every appliance on its own. <code>sha256sum -c payload_manifest.txt</code> inside
<code>/opt/aura</code> shows whether a host matches its payload. Watch it with
<code>journalctl -u aura-image-update.service -f</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Manual update (pinned installs, or no systemd). Volumes persist, and the
<code>aura-migrate</code> one-shot runs the Postgres migrations before the Aura service
starts (ArcadeDB needs none — the MCP creates each identity's database on first
use):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker compose pull
docker compose up -d"><pre>docker compose pull
docker compose up -d</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Backup And Restore</h2><a id="user-content-backup-and-restore" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Backup And Restore" href="#backup-and-restore"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Scheduled backups run inside the socketless Aura box. Postgres is dumped over the
Compose network with <code>pg_dump</code> into <code>AURA_BACKUP_DIR</code>:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./backups/postgres-YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ.dump"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>./backups/postgres-YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ.dump
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Memory is backed up automatically.</strong> ArcadeDB loads
<a href="/chetto1983/Aura/blob/master/docker/arcadedb/backup.json"><code>docker/arcadedb/backup.json</code></a> and backs up every
database every 60 minutes, including newly-created identity databases. Archives
live in the separate <code>aura-arcadedb-backups</code> volume. The configuration sets
<code>maxFiles=60</code> and tiered hourly/daily/weekly/monthly retention of 24/7/4/6.</p>
<p dir="auto">The database and backup volumes are separate, but both are on the same host by
default. Preserve off-host copies and the deployment configuration separately.
Garage objects, workspaces, and other runtime files need their own backup policy.</p>
<p dir="auto">Run the restore drill against the current Compose stack:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="set -a
. ./.env
set +a
scripts/restore_drill.sh"><pre><span class="pl-c1">set</span> -a
<span class="pl-c1">.</span> ./.env
<span class="pl-c1">set</span> +a
scripts/restore_drill.sh</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The drill tests four planes: Postgres, conversation sidecars, Garage and an
ArcadeDB database shaped like a tenant. It verifies restored checksums and cleans
up its disposable resources. All four passed on 2026-09-07. A separate restore
of an existing scheduled operator-memory archive recovered 93 entities, 75 facts
and 40 mentions, including a historical fact. This is a dated recovery check,
not a complete host-loss rehearsal or an RPO/RTO guarantee. See
<a href="/chetto1983/Aura/blob/master/docs/BACKUP-RESTORE.md">Backup and restore</a> for scope and evidence.</p>
<p dir="auto">Manual restore commands:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker compose exec -T -e PGPASSWORD=&quot;$POSTGRES_PASSWORD&quot; postgres \
  pg_restore -U &quot;${POSTGRES_USER:-aura}&quot; -d &quot;${POSTGRES_DB:-aura}&quot; \
  --clean --if-exists --no-owner --no-acl /backups/postgres-YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ.dump"><pre>docker compose <span class="pl-c1">exec</span> -T -e PGPASSWORD=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$POSTGRES_PASSWORD</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> postgres \
  pg_restore -U <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">${POSTGRES_USER<span class="pl-k">:-</span>aura}</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> -d <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">${POSTGRES_DB<span class="pl-k">:-</span>aura}</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> \
  --clean --if-exists --no-owner --no-acl /backups/postgres-YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ.dump</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Take a fresh backup before restoring over a live database.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">WhatsApp MCP</h2><a id="user-content-whatsapp-mcp" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: WhatsApp MCP" href="#whatsapp-mcp"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>whatsapp</code> service is part of the default stack, mounted through Aura's MCP catalog.
It uses an unofficial whatsmeow-based client, so it carries WhatsApp Terms of Service and account-ban risk. First pairing is headless:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker compose logs -f whatsapp"><pre>docker compose logs -f whatsapp</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Scan the QR code shown in the logs. Aura boot never depends on this service.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Retired Host Setup</h2><a id="user-content-retired-host-setup" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Retired Host Setup" href="#retired-host-setup"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The host needs no Python MCP runtime at all: memory is served by Aura's own
ArcadeDB MCP, a Go binary in the image. Old host-level Python installs and the
earlier WSL WhatsApp MCP install can be removed after migrating to the Compose
appliance.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CLI</h2><a id="user-content-cli" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CLI" href="#cli"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="aura serve                    run the long-lived agent runtime (channels, cockpit, scheduler)

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Development</h2><a id="user-content-development" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Development" href="#development"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For source builds, install Go from <a href="/chetto1983/Aura/blob/master/go.mod"><code>go.mod</code></a>, Docker, and a POSIX shell.
Linux is the supported source-build and quality-gate runtime. On Windows, use WSL;
the native Windows Go binary is not a release target because Windows ACLs are not
represented by POSIX <code>FileMode</code> bits. Docker Desktop remains supported for running
the shipped Linux Compose appliance.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/chetto1983/Aura.git
cd Aura
cp .env.example .env
make tools
lefthook install
make db-migrate memory-up
go run ./cmd/aura version
go run ./cmd/aura agent dry-run --request-id auto"><pre>git clone https://github.com/chetto1983/Aura.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> Aura
cp .env.example .env
make tools
lefthook install
make db-migrate memory-up
go run ./cmd/aura version
go run ./cmd/aura agent dry-run --request-id auto</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">To run a local source build instead of a published image, build
the image and point <code>.env</code> at it (the image builds
<code>web/</code> in its own stage; the committed <code>internal/webui/dist</code> only feeds a host <code>go build</code>
and is refreshed from that stage, never from a host <code>vite build</code>):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker build -f docker/aura/Dockerfile -t aura:local .
# then in .env:  AURA_IMAGE=aura:local"><pre>docker build -f docker/aura/Dockerfile -t aura:local <span class="pl-c1">.</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> then in .env:  AURA_IMAGE=aura:local</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Quality gates:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make quality
make db-migrate memory-up
make quality-full"><pre>make quality
make db-migrate memory-up
make quality-full</pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Project Layout</h2><a id="user-content-project-layout" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Project Layout" href="#project-layout"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cmd/aura/                CLI entry and subcommands

.planning/               GSD planning artifacts
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Scope</h2><a id="user-content-scope" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Scope" href="#scope"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Aura is PRD-first. Persistence is Postgres plus an ArcadeDB graph, with graph access
through MCP for model-facing tools. The default packaged deployment keeps Aura
socketless: no Docker socket is mounted into the Aura container. The opt-in <code>sandbox</code>
profile reaches Docker only through a socket proxy that allows the box lifecycle verbs.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Contributing And Security</h2><a id="user-content-contributing-and-security" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contributing And Security" href="#contributing-and-security"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="/chetto1983/Aura/blob/master/CONTRIBUTING.md"><code>CONTRIBUTING.md</code></a>. Report vulnerabilities privately per
<a href="/chetto1983/Aura/blob/master/SECURITY.md"><code>SECURITY.md</code></a>, never in a public issue.</p>
